# Lifted files that differ from upstream

Generated by `tools/lift/changes_report.py` from `tools/lift/manifests/`: each lifted file is compared with its upstream source at the lifted commit (line endings and byte-order marks ignored). Files not listed are byte-for-byte copies. The note is the `webForCore:` comment the change carries; the reasoning for each group of changes is in [MODIFICATIONS.md](MODIFICATIONS.md) and `LIFT.md`. Files that webForCore added rather than changed are under `tools/lift/additions/` and are not listed here.

**4,517 files lifted, 254 changed.**
